HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1357b4efdfdc9f68c44f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:4:3\",\"root_001088\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Örtme hareketi, sınır ihlali şiddete dönüştüğünde her şeyi kuşatan bir belaya dönüşme ihtimalini içinde taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_194fac120ab1a34448a2","text":"91:7-8 activated `غ ش و/B005` by supplying an interior that can have discernment obscured. 91:9 then corrected a possible overreading: night itself is not equated with failure; purification shows that the cover is a reversible model for opacity, not a moral verdict on darkness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2cb8435eb0893ef4c300","text":"3. **Visitation/coming-upon as the form of moral event.** Lexical evidence: غ ش و B003 \"coming upon or visiting\"; ب ع ث B002 \"dispatching and directing\" in 91:12; ر س ل B001/B002 sending and messenger in 91:13. Structural cue: night \"comes over\" the object, later the wretched one is roused and the messenger is sent. Assigned role: غشى supplies the grammar of an event arriving upon a scene. Abductive move: I infer that the narrative events come upon the people the way night comes upon the day-field; the packet supplies coming-upon, rousing, and sending. Reading change: the ayah frames later moral crisis as an arrival/visitation, not only an internal choice.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000476/B001","root_001088/B001","root_001290/B009","root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3d0c0c72268a88094686","text":"1. **Night as covering that makes concealment morally intelligible.** Lexical evidence: ل ي ل B001 \"night as opposite of day and its darkness\"; غ ش و B001 \"covering veil\"; د س و B001 \"hidden insertion or concealment\" in 91:10; ك ذ ب B009 \"deceptively variegated dyed cloth\" in 91:11/14. Structural cue: night covers after day unveils, and later failure is expressed as burying/covering the soul. Assigned role: night provides a neutral cosmic cover that later moral concealment imitates or abuses. Abductive move: I infer that the same material action, covering, can be innocent cosmically and corrupt morally; the packet supplies both cover and concealment branches. Reading change: the ayah becomes the morally ambiguous shadow-side of the disclosure sequence, not a negative symbol by itself.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_511d0faf8153d2d7946d","text":"91:14 retrospectively made غشى darker: the cover of night can become the engulfing totality of damdama when concealment and denial harden into violence. Still, the day/night pair kept the reading from making night evil by itself; the later crime shows the abusive human imitation of covering, not a condemnation of cosmic covering.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B001","root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8292ff3c3336e0d1df94","text":"1. **Night as an applied veil.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `غ ش و/B001` is a covering laid over sight, heart, garment, or object; fixed `ل ي ل/B001` supplies night and its darkness. **Structural cue and role:** the clause exactly reverses 91:3's `جلاها` while retaining `إذا` and the feminine object, so the veil is an event applied to what day had disclosed. **Abductive move:** reversal and shared object are supplied; construing darkness as an operation rather than a substance is inferred. **Reading change:** night reads as active concealment, not merely absence of day.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B001","root_001088/B005","root_001392/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_88b1e78260e5c55f5c1b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:4:1\",\"root_001392\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:4:3\",\"root_001088\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gecenin kozmik örtüsü, daha sonra bilgilendirilmiş canın kendi ayrımlarını örtmesini anlaşılır kılan tarafsız bir dış modeldir; gece bu nedenle başlı başına kötülük sayılmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_975a67308c32794d2e95","text":"2. **A model for obscured inner discernment.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `غ ش و/B005` is a covering of consciousness or understanding; 91:7 `ن ف س/B013` is inner mind and discernment, and 91:8 places two opposed contents within that self. **Structural cue and role:** cosmic covering precedes the formed and informed interior, giving the night-clause the role of a visible analogue for cognitive opacity. **Abductive move:** the outer-to-inner analogy is inferred; the packet does not say that night causes moral obscurity. **Reading change:** alongside literal nightfall, `يغشاها` makes intelligible how an already endowed self can have its distinctions covered over.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_aca69972b57413b8afad","text":"3. **Covering as a recurring visit, not permanent possession.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `غ ش و/B003` is coming upon or visiting a person or place. **Structural cue and role:** the repeated `إذا` clauses and the moon/day/night succession make covering one arrival within a reversible cycle; the visit branch assigns temporariness to its role. **Abductive move:** recurrence is explicit, while treating the night as a visitor is a branch-driven personification. **Reading change:** the ayah can present darkness as something that comes upon the field and then yields, preserving the possibility of renewed disclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d69a2f7b9a5eb116bf62","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:4:3\",\"root_001088\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gecenin gelişini bir ziyaret gibi okumak, örtünün kalıcı bir sahiplik değil, yeniden açılmaya yer bırakan geçici bir uğrak olduğunu düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000486/B001","root_001088/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_faec6fe4734f7ba298b9","text":"2. **Overwhelming affliction latent in the cover.** Lexical evidence: غ ش و B002 \"overwhelming affliction\"; د م د م B001 \"complete eradication\" in 91:14; ط غ ي B002 \"overflowing and overwhelming force\" in 91:11. Structural cue: a gentle night-covering early in the packet is later matched by overwhelming destruction. Assigned role: غشى carries a dormant disaster branch that later damdama activates. Abductive move: I supply the arrow from covering to engulfing: when bounds are violated, cover becomes total overtaking. Reading change: night covering acquires a latent threat: the same enveloping motion that can cloak can also crush.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
